Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The approximation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 303. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 472 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the approximation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^303 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 472-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any approximation module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 685. The projective resolution has length at most 330 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to approximation theory, we decompose the problem into 685 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 330. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
